Title: Материалы для истории раскола за первое время его существования, под редакцией Н. Субботина; том шестой: сочинения первых расколоучителей, часть третья: сочинения бывшего благовещенского соборного диакона Федора Иванова (vol. VI: the Works of the former Deacon of the Annunciation Cathedral, Fyodor Ivanov)
Creator: Deacon Fyodor Ivanov (d. 1682); edited by Nikolai Ivanovich Subbotin (1827-1905)
Publisher: Moscow: Bratstvo sv. Petra mitropolita
Publication Date: 1881
Rights: Public Domain (published 1881, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item carries no rights field, so the date rule alone applies)
Language: rus
Source: Internet Archive item materialydljaistoriiraskolazapervoevremj51 (https://archive.org/details/materialydljaistoriiraskolazapervoevremj51), the item's own materialydljaistoriiraskolazapervoevremj51_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: not named in the item metadata; host metadata date: 1881. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: 0436b42770a6c57cfe14caeceebb3ef0fd53057d3eef0a9b8bc3a200008073c9.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. Scan contributed to the Internet Archive without a named library; Cyrillic OCR good on the texts.
Content note: Deacon Fyodor's writings: his answer to the Orthodox, his letters to his son Maksim and his disputes with Avvakum and Lazar over doctrine in Pustozersk; the Pustozersk prisoners' own internal debate.
